the venue scrape. Regenerable, so do not edit it — change the override instead.

means omit a category label rather than invent a performer.

| 1984 | Julsång | Ser du stjärnan i det blå | Sven-Olof Sandberg | Ser du stjärnan i det blå? | Christmas | Sven-Olof Sandberg – Ser du stjärnan i det blå? Swedish of When You Wish Upon a Star (Pinocchio). Not a traditional carol: first Swedish record of this Halldén lyric is Sandberg with Willard Ringstrand, Sonora 7022, recorded 23 Jan 1941, issued Feb 1941 (SMDB). Cliff Edwards is the English original; the 1941 Swedish dub used a different lyric (När en önskestjärna tänds); Bertil Engh is the 1995 dub; Bengt Feldreich is the 16-second Kalle Anka clip, which is why Christmas stays. Year 1941 from that first Swedish release. | 50852 |
